# CLAUDE.md — Self-Maintaining APIs (CS 4398, Group 13)

Before any work, read `prompts/00_master_context.md` and `docs/PROGRESS.md`. Follow the Working Rules in the master context.

## 7. Working rules for every increment (MANDATORY)

Three people (Bigyan, Bibek, Sishir) build increments **in parallel**, each with Claude Code on their own machine. These rules keep parallel work from colliding.

1. **ONE capability per increment.** Do not start the next increment until told to.
2. **Start of every increment:** `git checkout main && git pull`, then `npm ci`, `npm run consumer:install`, and `npm run db:migrate`. Confirm that the increment's GitHub Issue is **assigned to the current user** (`gh issue view <n>`). If it is not, stop and say so.
3. Save the increment prompt **verbatim** to `prompts/NN_name.md` (if it is not already there).
4. **Branch:** `feat/incNN-short-name` from the latest `main`. Make small, meaningful commits.
5. Write unit tests for every Model/Controller-level class or function you add. All tests, typecheck, and lint must pass before the PR is opened.
6. Add TSDoc comments to exported functions/classes (TypeDoc builds from them).
7. **Don't stop to ask questions about small choices.** Make a sensible choice that fits the SRS, and log it in `docs/decisions/incNN.md` (one file per increment: date, decision, why). Stop and ask ONLY if a choice would break an SRS rule or is costly to undo.
8. Never commit secrets. Credentials come from `.env` (gitignored); only `.env.example` is committed. Never log or export credentials (NFR-04).
9. **Database schema changes:** make a NEW migration, created **after** merging the latest `main`. Never edit an existing migration. If two PRs both add migrations, the second one regenerates its migration after updating from `main`.
10. **Shared code:** only add to `packages/core` in a new file or with new exports. Do not rename or remove exports that other increments use without saying so in the PR.
11. **Progress:** the GitHub Issues board is the source of truth. Update this increment's one line in `docs/PROGRESS.md` in the increment's own PR only.
12. **Before opening the PR:** `git fetch && git merge origin/main` into the branch, fix any conflicts, and re-run typecheck, lint, and tests.
13. **End of every increment:** do NOT merge into `main` yourself. Push the branch and open a Pull Request with `gh pr create`. Title: `Increment NN: <name>`. Body: the INCREMENT REPORT, plus `Closes #<issue>`. A teammate merges it on GitHub with **"Create a merge commit"** (never squash or rebase, so the git Network graph keeps its branches).
14. **Chat logs:** at the end, the human runs `/export docs/chat-logs/incNN-<firstname>.txt`, and it is committed in the same PR.

## 8. End-of-increment report (MANDATORY format)

End every increment with exactly this:
```
INCREMENT NN REPORT
Status: DONE | PARTIAL | BLOCKED
Built: <bullets>
Tests: <passed>/<total>, typecheck: ok/fail, lint: ok/fail
Commands to verify: <bullets>
Decisions logged: <bullets>
SRS requirements covered: <IDs>
Problems / things the human must do: <bullets or "none">
```
# Team guide

Three of us (Bigyan, Bibek, Sishir) build the project **at the same time**, each with Claude Code
on our own Mac. Each piece of work is one **increment**, tracked as one **GitHub Issue**.

## 1. One-time setup on a Mac

1. Install Homebrew (see https://brew.sh), then in Terminal:
   ```bash
   brew install git node@22 oasdiff gh
   brew install --cask docker
   ```
2. Open the **Docker** app once and wait until it says it is running.
3. Install Claude Code and log in to GitHub:
   ```bash
   npm install -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code
   gh auth login
   ```

## 2. Get the project

```bash
gh repo clone Bigyan1313/self_maintaining_api
cd self_maintaining_api
cp .env.example .env        # then open .env and fill in your own values
npm ci
npm run consumer:install
npm run db:up               # starts the database (Docker must be running)
npm run db:migrate
npm test                    # everything should pass
```

## 3. Taking a step

1. Open the repo's **Issues** tab and pick an issue with the green **`ready`** label.
2. Click **Assign yourself** on it, so nobody else takes it.
3. Make sure its prompt file is in `prompts/` (download it there if it is missing).
4. In the project folder, run `claude` and type:
   ```
   Read prompts/NN_name.md and follow it exactly.
   ```
   Claude Code pulls the latest `main`, checks that the issue is assigned to you, and works on its
   own branch.

## 4. Finishing

1. Claude Code opens a **Pull Request** (it never merges by itself).
2. Save the chat into the PR: in Claude Code, run
   `/export docs/chat-logs/incNN-<yourname>.txt`, then ask Claude Code to commit and push it.
3. Ask a teammate to look at the PR.
4. Merge it on GitHub with **"Create a merge commit"**. Never use squash or rebase, because they
   break the branch graph the course wants to see.
5. Merging closes the issue. Any issue that was waiting on it turns `ready` automatically.

## 5. Rules

- Only work on an issue that is **assigned to you**.
- Never push straight to `main`. Everything goes through a Pull Request.
- Never commit `.env` (it holds your secrets).
- If you get stuck, post in the group chat instead of letting Claude Code guess on big changes.
# INCREMENT 04: Ingest (register, retrieve, normalize, hash, baseline)
Status: Standard
Dependencies: Increment 03 (database schema) merged

### CRITICAL — READ FIRST ###
**MANDATORY:** Follow the Working Rules in `CLAUDE.md` (team PR flow). Save this prompt verbatim to `prompts/04_ingest.md` first. Branch: `feat/inc04-ingest`. Requirement IDs refer to `docs/SRS.md`.
**CRITICAL:** A failed retrieval/parse is **NOT** "no change". It is a **failed check**, the baseline stays untouched, and it can be retried (FR-11). Never store a credential, only the **name** of the env var that holds it.
**CRITICAL:** This increment covers stage 1 (Ingest) and configuration. Do NOT diff, classify, or locate.

---

## Context for whoever picks this up
Already on `main`: monorepo + tooling (01), demo API with `demo/api/specs/v1.yaml` and `v2.yaml` (02), and the DB schema with `repositories`, `apis`, `spec_versions`, `spec_checks` and enums in `packages/core/src/domain/enums.ts` (03). Read those files before starting.

## Capability
A developer can register a repository and an API (FR-01) with validation (FR-02), then upload or retrieve an OpenAPI spec that gets normalized, hashed, stored, and compared by hash to set or keep the baseline (FR-03–05, FR-11, FR-12).

## Part A — Shared HTTP API contract (types only) — `packages/core/src/api/contract.ts`
**Every later increment and the dashboard code against this file.** Write request/response types (zod schemas + inferred types) for ALL of these, even ones implemented later. Mark each with a TSDoc `@implementedIn incNN` tag:
| Method | Path | Implemented in |
|---|---|---|
| GET/POST | `/api/repositories` | 04 |
| GET/POST | `/api/apis` | 04 |
| POST | `/api/apis/:id/specs` (upload file body) | 04 |
| POST | `/api/apis/:id/check` (retrieve from URL) | 04 |
| GET | `/api/apis/:id/checks` | 04 |
| POST | `/api/apis/:id/migration-notes` | 06 |
| GET | `/api/changes?apiId=` | 06 |
| POST | `/api/changes/:id/confirm-mapping` | 06 |
| POST | `/api/runs` `{ changeId, repositoryId }` | 13 |
| GET | `/api/runs`, `/api/runs/:id` (RunDetail: run, stage results, call sites, coverage, attempts, PR link or reason) | 13 |
| POST | `/api/runs/:id/reanalyze`, `/api/runs/:id/retry-delivery` | 13 |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/export?format=json\|html` | 13 |
| GET | `/api/results` (latest evaluation results) | 16 |
Every outcome field in a response includes both the enum value and its **text label** (from the label maps in core).

## Part B — Pure logic in `packages/core/src/ingest/`
- `parseSpec(text, filenameHint)` → accepts **JSON or YAML**; rejects >5 MiB; validates as **OpenAPI 3.0 or 3.1** (reuse the validator library from inc02); returns a typed error with the parser message on failure.
- `normalizeSpec(doc)` → canonical JSON (recursively sorted keys, no whitespace differences) so the same spec in YAML and JSON hashes the same.
- `hashSpec(canonical)` → SHA-256, lowercase hex.
- `decideCheckOutcome({ baselineHash | null, newHash, existingHashes })` → `baseline_established` | `no_change` | `changed`. Pure and fully unit-tested.
- `countRepoSize(repoPath)` → number of selected `.ts`/`.tsx` files and lines, with the exclusions from SRS 5.2 (node_modules, .git, dist/build output, `.env*`, credential files, symlinks NOT followed). Put it in `packages/core/src/repo/limits.ts` so Increment 07 reuses it. Limits: **≤ 200 files, ≤ 20,000 lines.**

## Part C — Server (`apps/server`)
- **Spec retrieval** `src/infra/spec-source/`: HTTPS GET (also allow `http://localhost` / `127.0.0.1` for the demo API); 15 s timeout; 5 MiB cap; treat the body as text only — **never execute anything in it**; return typed failures (`unreachable`, `timeout`, `too_large`, `parse_error`, `invalid_openapi`).
- **Ingest service** `src/pipeline/ingest.ts`: given an API and either uploaded text or the API's URL:
  1. parse → normalize → hash
  2. no baseline → store version, set `apis.baseline_version_id`, record spec_check `baseline_established`, report **"Baseline established"** with no changes (FR-04)
  3. hash already stored for this API → record `no_change`, **no new version** (FR-05)
  4. new hash → store version, record `changed` (with the new version id). **Do NOT move the baseline here.** Expose `advanceBaseline(apiId, versionId)` for the orchestrator (inc 13) to call after classification (UC-02 step 6).
  5. any failure → record spec_check `failed` with error_detail; baseline unchanged; no version stored (FR-11, NFR-02)
  - `source_mode`: `uploaded`, `retrieved`, or `fixture` (a demo spec loaded from `demo/api/specs/` → `fixture`, BR-06).
- **Configuration validation (FR-02)** `src/pipeline/config-validate.ts`: field-level errors for missing/invalid fields; repository reachable (local_path exists, is a git repo, default branch exists; if `credential_ref` names an env var that is set, also check `GET /repos/{owner}/{name}` with Octokit and report `unreachable` on failure); repo within size limits (Part B); spec URL parseable or uploaded file valid. Return **all** field errors at once. **No run starts while there are errors.**
- **Routes** (Fastify + zod from the contract): repositories, apis, specs upload, check, list checks. Errors come back as `{ field, message }[]`.
- **Optional poller (FR-12):** off by default; when `pollIntervalSeconds ≥ 60` is set on an API, run checks **one at a time** (simple in-process queue). Unit-test the "≥ 60 s" and "one at a time" rules.

## Acceptance checks (AT-01, AT-02)
- [ ] Unit (core): YAML and JSON forms of `v1.yaml` give the **same hash**; v1 vs v2 hashes differ; >5 MiB rejected; Swagger 2.0 / garbage rejected with a parser message; `decideCheckOutcome` covers all 3 outcomes; `countRepoSize` respects exclusions and does not follow symlinks
- [ ] Integration (sma_test DB, `app.inject()`):
  - upload v1 → "Baseline established", 1 version, baseline set
  - upload v1 again (as JSON) → `no_change`, still 1 version
  - upload v2 → `changed`, 2 versions, **baseline still v1**
  - URL check against an unreachable host → spec_check `failed`, baseline unchanged, then a retry against the running demo API succeeds
- [ ] Registration with missing fields, unreachable repo path, bad spec, or an oversized repo → each returns field-level errors and **no** run/check row is created
- [ ] No column, log line, or response contains a token value (test with a fake `GITHUB_TOKEN=[REDACTED token-like value]` set: grep the logs and responses)
- [ ] Root typecheck, lint, and tests pass; CI green
- [ ] PR opened per the Working Rules (`Closes #<issue>`)

### CRITICAL — REMINDER ###
**MANDATORY:** Failed check ≠ no change; baseline never changes on failure; identical hash = no new version; the baseline is NOT advanced in this increment. The contract file covers ALL endpoints. Only env var *names* are stored. Decisions go in `docs/decisions/inc04.md`. End with the **INCREMENT 04 REPORT** and open the PR.

# PROMPT 00 — MASTER CONTEXT: Self-Maintaining APIs (CS 4398, Group 13)

### CRITICAL — READ FIRST ###
**MANDATORY:** This is the master context for the whole project. It is built with the **Module of Thought (MoT)** method: one master context plus one prompt per capability ("increment").
**MANDATORY:** Do the following now, and nothing else:
1. Save this entire prompt **verbatim** to `prompts/00_master_context.md`.
2. Create `CLAUDE.md` in the repo root. It must say: "Before any work, read `prompts/00_master_context.md` and `docs/PROGRESS.md`. Follow the Working Rules in the master context." Copy Sections 7 and 8 of this prompt into it as well.
3. Create `docs/PROGRESS.md` with the increment table from Section 9 (all rows "Not started").
4. Create `docs/DECISIONS.md` (empty log, with a heading).
5. **Do NOT write any application code yet.** Increment 01 comes in the next prompt.
6. Reply with a short confirmation and the file list.

---

## 1. Domain background

Applications call external REST APIs. Providers change their **OpenAPI** specs: endpoints get removed, fields get renamed, types change, and new required parameters are added. Today a developer has to notice the change, find the affected code, fix it, and prove the fix works. Existing tools cover only one piece of that:
- Dependabot/Renovate bump versions but don't change code.
- OpenRewrite/ast-grep need a hand-written rule.
- Spec-diff tools report changes but don't fix consumer code.

**Our system joins the whole path** (spec history → validated fix in the consumer's code → pull request) **and measures** how often an LLM agent repairs a breaking change correctly, and where it fails.

The full requirements are in `docs/SRS.docx` (Group 13 SRS v1.0). **Requirement IDs (FR-xx, NFR-xx, DR-xx, BR-xx, C-xx, EV-xx, AT-xx) refer to that document.** When a prompt cites an ID, read that part of the SRS.

## 2. System architecture — 7-stage pipeline

Each stage **writes its result to the database before the next stage begins** (FR-38). Runs are **append-only** (FR-39).

| # | Stage | Input | Output recorded |
|---|-------|-------|-----------------|
| 1 | Ingest | Spec URL or uploaded file | Spec version, normalized JSON, SHA-256 hash |
| 2 | Detect | Two spec versions | Structured diff list (path, method, JSON pointer, before/after evidence) |
| 3 | Classify | One difference | breaking / safe / unknown, confidence 0–1, mapping status |
| 4 | Locate | Change + repo | Call sites (file, line, column, pattern, excerpt) + coverage report |
| 5 | Repair | Change, sites, migration note, prior errors | Patch (unified diff), tokens, cost, model, attempt # |
| 6 | Validate | Patch + isolated working copy | pass / fail / cheating (+ guard name) |
| 7 | Deliver | Validated patch | PR URL, or flagged finding + reason |

**THE RULE THAT IS NEVER BROKEN (BR-04, C-04):** A spec difference is NOT proof that two fields mean the same thing. Stage 5 (Repair) runs **only** when the old→new mapping is **confirmed by a migration note or a human**. Everything else stops at stage 4 and is reported.

## 3. Component specifications

- **Scope (C-01):** TypeScript consumer repos only. 4 change categories: `endpoint_removed`, `field_renamed`, `field_type_changed`, `required_param_added`. 3 call patterns: `fetch` with a literal/template URL, `axios` via static import or a configured instance, generated-client function calls. Dynamic URLs, wrappers, and re-exported aliases → reported as **coverage limits / "Needs review"**, never treated as "not affected".
- **Detection:** uses **oasdiff** (Go binary, installed locally, version pinned). The oasdiff *runner* lives in infra. The *parser/classifier* lives in `packages/core` and takes oasdiff JSON as input, so it is unit-testable with fixtures and needs no network, container, or model (NFR-08).
- **Migration note:** a structured YAML file (format defined in the Classify increment), e.g. `mappings: [{kind: field_renamed, method: GET, path: /orders/{id}, old: customer_name, new: full_name}]`.
- **Location:** **ts-morph** syntax-tree analysis. Never plain text search.
- **Repair:** the LLM gets ONLY: the changed spec section, the migration note, the files containing call sites, and validator errors from earlier attempts (FR-19). **Max 4 attempts** per run (FR-21, DR-03). Token ceiling per run → over the limit = `aborted` (FR-24).
- **LLM adapter (C-06):** ALL model calls go through ONE file, `apps/server/src/infra/llm/adapter.ts`. The provider is swappable by editing only that file.
- **Validation (FR-25, NFR-03):** Docker container, `--network none`, non-root user, memory/CPU limits, 10-minute wall clock, image **pinned by digest**. Runs the repo's own typecheck + tests (Vitest), unchanged. Generated code **never** runs on the host (C-03).
- **Cheat guards (FR-27–31):** reject a patch that (a) adds/edits/deletes/skips any test file, (b) lowers the passing-test count or changes the total-test count, (c) adds `@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error`/`@ts-nocheck`, an unchecked `any`, or an empty `catch` in its changed lines, (d) edits tsconfig/vitest/package config instead of source. Record which guard fired. A cheating result **ends the run immediately**.
- **Delivery (FR-33–37, C-02):** Octokit: push a branch + open a PR labeled machine-generated / awaiting review. **NEVER merge, never enable auto-merge, never push to the default branch.**
- **Dashboard (UI-01–04):** Configuration, Changes, Runs, Run Detail, Results views. **Every outcome state has a text label, not just a color** (FR-42).
- **Evaluation (Section 6 of the SRS — as important as the code):** 20 frozen scenarios (5 per category; 3 endpoint-removals with no replacement, where refusing is the correct answer). Baselines: B1 do nothing, B2 one LLM call with no validation/retries/guards, B3 full pipeline. The script writes a machine-readable results file; tables are generated from it, never typed by hand (EV-08).

## 4. Tech stack (pin EXACT versions — no `^` or `~`, C-07)

- Node 22 LTS, TypeScript (strict), **npm workspaces**
- Server: Fastify + zod. DB: PostgreSQL 16 (docker-compose) + Drizzle ORM + drizzle-kit migrations
- Web: React + Vite
- Tests: Vitest. Lint: ESLint + Prettier. API docs: **TypeDoc** (stands in for javadoc in the course deliverables)
- ts-morph, oasdiff (binary), `yaml`, Octokit, `@anthropic-ai/sdk` (behind the adapter only)
- Docker for validation. GitHub Actions CI.

## 5. Repository layout

```
self-maintaining-apis/
  CLAUDE.md
  prompts/                 # every MoT prompt, saved verbatim (course deliverable #1)
  docs/
    SRS.docx  PROGRESS.md  DECISIONS.md
    diagrams/              # class diagram + pipeline statechart (Mermaid/PlantUML)
    acceptance/            # AT-01..AT-12 execution records + screenshots
  packages/core/           # PURE domain logic: ingest, detect-parse, classify, locate, guards, types
  apps/server/             # Fastify HTTP + pipeline orchestrator
    src/infra/             # db, llm adapter, docker validator, github, oasdiff runner
  apps/web/                # React dashboard
  demo/api/                # demo REST API served at v1 and v2 + openapi specs + migration notes
  demo/consumer/           # sample TypeScript repo that calls the demo API
  scenarios/               # 20 frozen evaluation scenarios
  eval/                    # evaluation script + results
```
**C-05:** domain logic, HTTP handling, and presentation stay separate. `packages/core` must NOT import from `apps/*`, and must NOT touch the network, Docker, or the LLM.

## 6. Performance thresholds (quantitative tracking)

| Metric | Green | Yellow | Red |
|---|---|---|---|
| Detect+classify+locate time (≤200 files / 20k lines, NFR-05) | < 30 s | 30–60 s | > 60 s |
| Unit tests passing | 100% | — | < 100% |
| Model request | < 60 s | 60–120 s | timeout at 120 s (NFR-06) |
| Container validation | < 5 min | 5–10 min | timeout at 10 min |
| Attempts per successful repair | 1–2 | 3–4 | > 4 (impossible by DR-03) |

## 7. Working rules for every increment (MANDATORY)

Three people (Bigyan, Bibek, Sishir) build increments **in parallel**, each with Claude Code on their own machine. These rules keep parallel work from colliding.

1. **ONE capability per increment.** Do not start the next increment until told to.
2. **Start of every increment:** `git checkout main && git pull`, then `npm ci`, `npm run consumer:install`, and `npm run db:migrate`. Confirm that the increment's GitHub Issue is **assigned to the current user** (`gh issue view <n>`). If it is not, stop and say so.
3. Save the increment prompt **verbatim** to `prompts/NN_name.md` (if it is not already there).
4. **Branch:** `feat/incNN-short-name` from the latest `main`. Make small, meaningful commits.
5. Write unit tests for every Model/Controller-level class or function you add. All tests, typecheck, and lint must pass before the PR is opened.
6. Add TSDoc comments to exported functions/classes (TypeDoc builds from them).
7. **Don't stop to ask questions about small choices.** Make a sensible choice that fits the SRS, and log it in `docs/decisions/incNN.md` (one file per increment: date, decision, why). Stop and ask ONLY if a choice would break an SRS rule or is costly to undo.
8. Never commit secrets. Credentials come from `.env` (gitignored); only `.env.example` is committed. Never log or export credentials (NFR-04).
9. **Database schema changes:** make a NEW migration, created **after** merging the latest `main`. Never edit an existing migration. If two PRs both add migrations, the second one regenerates its migration after updating from `main`.
10. **Shared code:** only add to `packages/core` in a new file or with new exports. Do not rename or remove exports that other increments use without saying so in the PR.
11. **Progress:** the GitHub Issues board is the source of truth. Update this increment's one line in `docs/PROGRESS.md` in the increment's own PR only.
12. **Before opening the PR:** `git fetch && git merge origin/main` into the branch, fix any conflicts, and re-run typecheck, lint, and tests.
13. **End of every increment:** do NOT merge into `main` yourself. Push the branch and open a Pull Request with `gh pr create`. Title: `Increment NN: <name>`. Body: the INCREMENT REPORT, plus `Closes #<issue>`. A teammate merges it on GitHub with **"Create a merge commit"** (never squash or rebase, so the git Network graph keeps its branches).
14. **Chat logs:** at the end, the human runs `/export docs/chat-logs/incNN-<firstname>.txt`, and it is committed in the same PR.

Detection answers one question: what is different between two versions of this API, and which differences will break code? A difference that cannot be attributed to a specific API item is reported as unknown rather than guessed at.

| **ID** | **Requirement** | **Verification** |

| FR-08 | The system shall retain the before and after evidence for every difference, so a classification can be checked against the specification text that produced it. | AT-03, AT-08 |

## 5  Design and implementation constraints

### 5.1  Constraints

- C-01: The release shall analyze TypeScript repositories only, using the call patterns and change categories defined in Section 5.2.
- C-02: The system shall never merge a pull request, enable automatic merging, push to a default branch, deploy, or modify the developer’s working copy.
- C-03: Generated code shall execute only inside the validation container, never on the host.
- C-04: The system shall repair only a change whose mapping is confirmed by a migration note or a human. It shall not infer an undocumented mapping, and it shall not claim to understand arbitrary API documentation.
- C-05: The implementation shall separate domain logic, HTTP handling, and presentation. Detection, classification, and location shall each be testable without the browser, the container, or a live model.
- C-06: All model calls shall pass through one adapter module with a pinned provider version, so that replacing the provider touches exactly one file.
- C-07: Runtime, dependency, and container-image versions shall be pinned in the release installation files.

The implementation baseline is TypeScript [2] on Node, specification-aware differencing of OpenAPI documents [1], [4], a TypeScript syntax-tree library for call-site location [3], a relational database for run records [8], a container runtime for sandboxed validation [5] running the project’s type check and test suite [6], and the GitHub REST API for delivery [7]. Exact versions are pinned per C-07.

### 5.2  Supported inputs and operating limits

| **Area** | **Release limit** |
|---|---|
| Specification | One OpenAPI 3.0 or 3.1 document per API, JSON or YAML, at most 5 MiB. A document that fails schema validation is rejected with the parser error. |
| Change categories | Endpoint removed; field renamed; field type changed; required parameter added. A repair is offered only when the old-to-new mapping is documented. Response-shape and authentication migrations are outside this release. |
| Call patterns | Direct fetch calls with a literal or template-literal URL; axios calls through a static import or a configured instance; generated-client function calls. Dynamically constructed URLs, wrappers, and re-exported aliases are unsupported and shall be reported as coverage limits. |
| Repository limits | At most 200 selected TypeScript files and 20,000 selected source lines. Symbolic links are not followed. Dependency directories, version-control metadata, build output, and credential or environment files are excluded. Oversized input is refused with a clear limit message. |
| Validation container | A pinned image digest, no network, non-root, memory and CPU ceilings, and a 10-minute wall-clock limit. The repository’s own type-check and test commands are used unchanged. |
| Repair loop | At most four attempts per run, with a configured token ceiling per run. Validator output from the previous attempt is included in the next context. |

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parse } from 'yaml';
import type { DemoVersion } from '../src/data.js';

/** Minimal view of an OpenAPI document used by the tests. */
export interface OpenApiDoc {
  openapi: string;
  info: { version: string };
  paths: Record<
    string,
    Record<string, { operationId: string; responses: Record<string, ResponseObj> }>
  >;
  components: { responses?: Record<string, ResponseObj> };
}

interface ResponseObj {
  $ref?: string;
  content?: Record<string, { schema: unknown }>;
}

/** Loads `specs/<version>.yaml` as a plain object. */
export function loadSpec(version: DemoVersion): OpenApiDoc {
  const file = fileURLToPath(new URL(`../specs/${version}.yaml`, import.meta.url));
  return parse(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')) as OpenApiDoc;
}

const escapeSegment = (s: string): string =>
  encodeURIComponent(s.replace(/~/g, '~0').replace(/\//g, '~1'));

/**
 * JSON-pointer fragment (URI-encoded) to the JSON response schema for one operation/status,
 * following a `$ref` to `components/responses` when present. `undefined` when no body is defined.
 */
export function responseSchemaPointer(
  spec: OpenApiDoc,
  path: string,
  method: string,
  status: string,
): string | undefined {
  const response = spec.paths[path]?.[method]?.responses[status];
  if (!response) throw new Error(`Spec has no ${status} response for ${method} ${path}`);

  let segments = ['paths', path, method, 'responses', status];
  let target = response;
  if (response.$ref) {
    const name = response.$ref.split('/').at(-1) ?? '';
    const resolved = spec.components.responses?.[name];
    if (!resolved) throw new Error(`Unresolved ${response.$ref}`);
    segments = ['components', 'responses', name];
    target = resolved;
  }
  if (!target.content?.['application/json']) return undefined;
  return `#/${[...segments, 'content', 'application/json', 'schema'].map(escapeSegment).join('/')}`;
}

import { drizzle, type NodePgDatabase } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres';
import pg from 'pg';
import * as schema from './schema.js';

/** Typed Drizzle database handle used by every repository module. */
export type Db = NodePgDatabase<typeof schema>;

/** A database handle together with the pool that backs it. */
export interface DbClient {
  db: Db;
  pool: pg.Pool;
  /** Closes every pooled connection. */
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Creates a pooled PostgreSQL client. The connection string is never logged (NFR-04).
 *
 * @param connectionString - Postgres URL; defaults to `process.env.DATABASE_URL`.
 * @throws Error when no connection string is available.
 */
export function createDbClient(connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL): DbClient {
  if (!connectionString) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is not set');
  const pool = new pg.Pool({ connectionString, max: 10 });
  const db = drizzle(pool, { schema });
  return { db, pool, close: () => pool.end() };
}
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { one } from './rows.js';
import { apis } from './schema.js';
import type { Api, NewApi } from './types.js';

/**
 * Inserts an API.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param values - API values.
 */
export async function insertApi(db: Db, values: NewApi): Promise<Api> {
  return one(await db.insert(apis).values(values).returning(), 'api');
}

/**
 * Finds an API by id.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param id - API id.
 */
export async function getApiById(db: Db, id: string): Promise<Api | undefined> {
  return (await db.select().from(apis).where(eq(apis.id, id)))[0];
}

/**
 * Finds an API by its unique name.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param name - API name.
 */
export async function getApiByName(db: Db, name: string): Promise<Api | undefined> {
  return (await db.select().from(apis).where(eq(apis.name, name)))[0];
}

/**
 * Sets the API's current baseline spec version.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param apiId - API id.
 * @param specVersionId - The new baseline version id.
 */
export async function setBaselineVersion(
  db: Db,
  apiId: string,
  specVersionId: string,
): Promise<Api> {
  return one(
    await db
      .update(apis)
      .set({ baselineVersionId: specVersionId })
      .where(eq(apis.id, apiId))
      .returning(),
    'api',
  );
}
import { and, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { one } from './rows.js';
import { repositories } from './schema.js';
import type { NewRepository, Repository } from './types.js';

/**
 * Inserts a repository. `credentialRef` must be an env var NAME (the DB rejects anything else).
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param values - Repository values.
 */
export async function insertRepository(db: Db, values: NewRepository): Promise<Repository> {
  return one(await db.insert(repositories).values(values).returning(), 'repository');
}

/**
 * Finds a repository by id.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param id - Repository id.
 */
export async function getRepositoryById(db: Db, id: string): Promise<Repository | undefined> {
  return (await db.select().from(repositories).where(eq(repositories.id, id)))[0];
}

/**
 * Finds a repository by owner and name.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param owner - Repository owner.
 * @param name - Repository name.
 */
export async function findRepository(
  db: Db,
  owner: string,
  name: string,
): Promise<Repository | undefined> {
  return (
    await db
      .select()
      .from(repositories)
      .where(and(eq(repositories.owner, owner), eq(repositories.name, name)))
  )[0];
}
import { and, asc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { one } from './rows.js';
import { specVersions } from './schema.js';
import type { NewSpecVersion, SpecVersion } from './types.js';

/**
 * Inserts a spec version. A duplicate `(apiId, contentSha256)` is rejected by the DB (FR-05).
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param values - Spec version values.
 */
export async function insertSpecVersion(db: Db, values: NewSpecVersion): Promise<SpecVersion> {
  return one(await db.insert(specVersions).values(values).returning(), 'spec version');
}

/**
 * Finds a spec version by id.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param id - Spec version id.
 */
export async function getSpecVersionById(db: Db, id: string): Promise<SpecVersion | undefined> {
  return (await db.select().from(specVersions).where(eq(specVersions.id, id)))[0];
}

/**
 * Finds the version of an API with a given content hash.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param apiId - API id.
 * @param contentSha256 - Lowercase hex SHA-256 of the normalized content.
 */
export async function findSpecVersionByHash(
  db: Db,
  apiId: string,
  contentSha256: string,
): Promise<SpecVersion | undefined> {
  return (
    await db
      .select()
      .from(specVersions)
      .where(and(eq(specVersions.apiId, apiId), eq(specVersions.contentSha256, contentSha256)))
  )[0];
}

/**
 * Lists an API's spec versions, oldest first.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param apiId - API id.
 */
export async function listSpecVersions(db: Db, apiId: string): Promise<SpecVersion[]> {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(specVersions)
    .where(eq(specVersions.apiId, apiId))
    .orderBy(asc(specVersions.retrievedAt), asc(specVersions.id));
}
import { desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { one } from './rows.js';
import { specChecks } from './schema.js';
import type { NewSpecCheck, SpecCheck } from './types.js';

/**
 * Records a spec check. A `failed` outcome requires `errorDetail` (FR-11, enforced by the DB).
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param values - Spec check values.
 */
export async function insertSpecCheck(db: Db, values: NewSpecCheck): Promise<SpecCheck> {
  return one(await db.insert(specChecks).values(values).returning(), 'spec check');
}

/**
 * Lists an API's spec checks, newest first.
 * @param db - Database handle.
 * @param apiId - API id.
 */
export async function listSpecChecks(db: Db, apiId: string): Promise<SpecCheck[]> {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(specChecks)
    .where(eq(specChecks.apiId, apiId))
    .orderBy(desc(specChecks.startedAt));
}

/**
 * Loads the repo-root `.env` into `process.env` when the file exists (local development).
 * In CI the variables come from the environment instead. Values are never printed.
 */
export function loadRootEnv(): void {
  const envFile = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../../../.env', import.meta.url));
  if (existsSync(envFile)) process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
}

/** Opens a client on the `sma_test` database. */
export function openTestDb(): DbClient {
  loadRootEnv();
  return createDbClient(testDatabaseUrl());
}

/** Lowercase hex SHA-256 of a random value (unique per call). */
export function randomSha256(): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(randomUUID()).digest('hex');
}

/** A random 40-char git commit SHA. */
export function randomCommitSha(): string {
  return createHash('sha1').update(randomUUID()).digest('hex');
}

/** Unique suffix so tests never collide (the append-only tables cannot be cleaned up). */
export const uid = (): string => randomUUID().slice(0, 8);

/** Postgres error details, unwrapped from Drizzle's wrapper error. */
interface PgErrorLike {
  code?: string;
  constraint?: string;
  message?: string;
  cause?: unknown;
}

function findPgError(error: unknown): PgErrorLike | undefined {
  let current: unknown = error;
  for (let depth = 0; depth < 5 && current; depth++) {
    const e = current as PgErrorLike;
    if (typeof e.code === 'string') return e;
    current = e.cause;
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** SQLSTATE codes asserted by the tests. */
export const PG = {
  checkViolation: '23514',
  uniqueViolation: '23505',
  raiseException: 'P0001',
} as const;

/**
 * Asserts that a query is rejected by the database with a given SQLSTATE (and, optionally, a
 * specific constraint name or message fragment).
 */
export async function expectDbRejects(
  promise: Promise<unknown>,
  expected: { code: string; constraint?: string; message?: RegExp },
): Promise<void> {
  let caught: unknown;
  try {
    await promise;
  } catch (error) {
    caught = error;
  }
  expect(caught, 'expected the database to reject the query').toBeDefined();
  const pgError = findPgError(caught);
  expect(pgError?.code, String((caught as Error | undefined)?.message)).toBe(expected.code);
  if (expected.constraint) expect(pgError?.constraint).toBe(expected.constraint);
  if (expected.message) expect(pgError?.message).toMatch(expected.message);
}

/** Records created by {@link seedChain}. */
export interface Chain {
  repository: Repository;
  api: Api;
  v1: SpecVersion;
  v2: SpecVersion;
  change: Change;
  run: Run;
}

/** Inserts repository → api → 2 spec versions → change → running run, all unique. */
export async function seedChain(client: DbClient): Promise<Chain> {
  const { db } = client;
  const s = uid();
  const repository = await insertRepository(db, {
    owner: 'acme',
    name: `consumer-${s}`,
    defaultBranch: 'main',
    credentialRef: 'GITHUB_TOKEN',
    localPath: `/tmp/consumer-${s}`,
  });
  const api = await insertApi(db, { name: `demo-shop-${s}`, pollIntervalSeconds: 300 });
  const v1 = await insertSpecVersion(db, {
    apiId: api.id,
    sourceMode: 'fixture',
    sourceIdentifier: 'demo/api/specs/v1.yaml',
    contentSha256: randomSha256(),
    normalized: { openapi: '3.1.0', info: { version: '1.0.0' } },
  });
  const v2 = await insertSpecVersion(db, {
    apiId: api.id,
    sourceMode: 'fixture',
    sourceIdentifier: 'demo/api/specs/v2.yaml',
    contentSha256: randomSha256(),
    normalized: { openapi: '3.1.0', info: { version: '2.0.0' } },
  });
  const change = await insertChange(db, {
    apiId: api.id,
    fromVersionId: v1.id,
    toVersionId: v2.id,
    category: 'field_renamed',
    oasdiffRuleId: 'response-required-property-removed',
    classification: 'breaking',
    confidence: 0.9,
    path: '/customers/{id}',
    method: 'GET',
    jsonPointer: '/paths/~1customers~1{id}/get/responses/200',
    evidenceBefore: { customer_name: { type: 'string' } },
    evidenceAfter: { full_name: { type: 'string' } },
  });
  const run = await insertRun(db, {
    changeId: change.id,
    repositoryId: repository.id,
    commitSha: randomCommitSha(),
    stageReached: 'locate',
    isFixture: true,
  });
  return { repository, api, v1, v2, change, run };
}

/** Minimal valid attempt values for a run. */
export function attemptValues(runId: string, attemptNumber: number) {
  return {
    runId,
    attemptNumber,
    patch: '--- a/src/customers.ts\n+++ b/src/customers.ts\n',
    validationOutput: 'ok',
    compileErrors: 0,
    testsPassed: 12,
    testsTotal: 12,
    outcome: 'fail' as const,
    tokensIn: 1000,
    tokensOut: 200,
    costUsd: '0.004200',
    modelId: 'test-model',
    durationMs: 1234,
  };
}

import { Validator } from '@seriousme/openapi-schema-validator';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loadSpec } from './spec-helpers.js';

describe.each(['v1', 'v2'] as const)('specs/%s.yaml', (version) => {
  const spec = loadSpec(version);

  it('is a valid OpenAPI 3.1 document', async () => {
    const validator = new Validator();
    const result = await validator.validate(
      spec as unknown as Parameters<Validator['validate']>[0],
    );
    expect(result.errors ?? []).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(spec.openapi).toMatch(/^3\.1\./);
  });

  it('gives every operation a unique operationId', () => {
    const ids = Object.values(spec.paths).flatMap((item) =>
      Object.values(item).map((op) => op.operationId),
    );
    expect(ids.every((id) => typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });
});
/** Successful outcome carrying a value of type `T`. */
export interface Ok<T> {
  readonly ok: true;
  readonly value: T;
}

/** Failed outcome carrying an error of type `E`. */
export interface Err<E> {
  readonly ok: false;
  readonly error: E;
}

/**
 * Outcome of an operation that can fail in an expected way.
 *
 * Domain functions return a `Result` instead of throwing, so callers must
 * handle the failure branch explicitly.
 */
export type Result<T, E> = Ok<T> | Err<E>;

/**
 * Builds a successful {@link Result}.
 * @param value - The success value.
 */
export function ok<T>(value: T): Ok<T> {
  return { ok: true, value };
}

/**
 * Builds a failed {@link Result}.
 * @param error - The error value.
 */
export function err<E>(error: E): Err<E> {
  return { ok: false, error };
}

/**
 * Type guard: `true` when the result is a success.
 * @param result - The result to inspect.
 */
export function isOk<T, E>(result: Result<T, E>): result is Ok<T> {
  return result.ok;
}

/**
 * Type guard: `true` when the result is a failure.
 * @param result - The result to inspect.
 */
export function isErr<T, E>(result: Result<T, E>): result is Err<E> {
  return !result.ok;
}
/**
 * `@sma/core` — pure domain logic for the Self-Maintaining APIs pipeline.
 *
 * This package must never import from `apps/*` and must never touch the
 * network, Docker, or an LLM (C-05, NFR-08).
 *
 * @packageDocumentation
 */

export { version } from './version.js';
export { ok, err, isOk, isErr } from './result.js';
export type { Result, Ok, Err } from './result.js';
export * from './domain/enums.js';
/**
 * Drizzle schema for Self-Maintaining APIs (SRS 2.4, 3.9; DR-01 to DR-04).
 *
 * Data rules are enforced by the database itself (CHECK, UNIQUE, foreign keys, and the
 * append-only triggers in migration `0001_append_only_triggers.sql`), not only by app code.
 *
 * @packageDocumentation
 */
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  boolean,
  check,
  doublePrecision,
  index,
  integer,
  jsonb,
  numeric,
  pgEnum,
  pgTable,
  text,
  timestamp,
  unique,
  uuid,
  type AnyPgColumn,
} from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import {
  ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES,
  BASELINES,
  CALL_PATTERNS,
  CHANGE_CATEGORIES,
  CLASSIFICATIONS,
  GUARD_NAMES,
  MAPPING_SOURCES,
  MAPPING_STATUSES,
  REVIEW_VERDICTS,
  RUN_STATUSES,
  SOURCE_MODES,
  SPEC_CHECK_OUTCOMES,
  STAGE_RESULT_STATUSES,
  STAGES,
} from '@sma/core';

// ---------- enums (built from the shared core arrays) ----------

/** pgEnum for `ChangeCategory`. */
export const changeCategoryEnum = pgEnum('change_category', CHANGE_CATEGORIES);
/** pgEnum for `Classification`. */
export const classificationEnum = pgEnum('classification', CLASSIFICATIONS);
/** pgEnum for `MappingStatus`. */
export const mappingStatusEnum = pgEnum('mapping_status', MAPPING_STATUSES);
/** pgEnum for `MappingSource`. */
export const mappingSourceEnum = pgEnum('mapping_source', MAPPING_SOURCES);
/** pgEnum for `SourceMode`. */
export const sourceModeEnum = pgEnum('source_mode', SOURCE_MODES);
/** pgEnum for `Stage`. */
export const stageEnum = pgEnum('stage', STAGES);
/** pgEnum for `StageResultStatus`. */
export const stageResultStatusEnum = pgEnum('stage_result_status', STAGE_RESULT_STATUSES);
/** pgEnum for `RunStatus`. */
export const runStatusEnum = pgEnum('run_status', RUN_STATUSES);
/** pgEnum for `AttemptOutcome`. */
export const attemptOutcomeEnum = pgEnum('attempt_outcome', ATTEMPT_OUTCOMES);
/** pgEnum for `GuardName`. */
export const guardNameEnum = pgEnum('guard_name', GUARD_NAMES);
/** pgEnum for `CallPattern`. */
export const callPatternEnum = pgEnum('call_pattern', CALL_PATTERNS);
/** pgEnum for `SpecCheckOutcome`. */
export const specCheckOutcomeEnum = pgEnum('spec_check_outcome', SPEC_CHECK_OUTCOMES);
/** pgEnum for `Baseline`. */
export const baselineEnum = pgEnum('baseline', BASELINES);
/** pgEnum for `ReviewVerdict`. */
export const reviewVerdictEnum = pgEnum('review_verdict', REVIEW_VERDICTS);

// ---------- column helpers ----------

const id = () => uuid('id').primaryKey().defaultRandom();
const tstz = (name: string) => timestamp(name, { withTimezone: true, mode: 'date' });
const money = (name: string) => numeric(name, { precision: 12, scale: 6 });

// ---------- tables ----------

/** Consumer repositories. `credential_ref` is the NAME of an env var, never a secret (NFR-04). */
export const repositories = pgTable(
  'repositories',
  {
    id: id(),
    owner: text('owner').notNull(),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    defaultBranch: text('default_branch').notNull(),
    credentialRef: text('credential_ref'),
    localPath: text('local_path'),
    createdAt: tstz('created_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('repositories_owner_name_uq').on(t.owner, t.name),
    check(
      'repositories_credential_ref_is_env_var_name',
      sql`${t.credentialRef} IS NULL OR ${t.credentialRef} ~ '^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*$'`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** Monitored APIs and their current baseline version. */
export const apis = pgTable(
  'apis',
  {
    id: id(),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    specSourceUrl: text('spec_source_url'),
    pollIntervalSeconds: integer('poll_interval_seconds'),
    baselineVersionId: uuid('baseline_version_id').references((): AnyPgColumn => specVersions.id),
    createdAt: tstz('created_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('apis_name_uq').on(t.name),
    check(
      'apis_poll_interval_min_60',
      sql`${t.pollIntervalSeconds} IS NULL OR ${t.pollIntervalSeconds} >= 60`,
    ),
    index('apis_baseline_version_id_idx').on(t.baselineVersionId),
  ],
);

/** Distinct spec versions per API (FR-05). Append-only. */
export const specVersions = pgTable(
  'spec_versions',
  {
    id: id(),
    apiId: uuid('api_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => apis.id),
    retrievedAt: tstz('retrieved_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
    sourceMode: sourceModeEnum('source_mode').notNull(),
    sourceIdentifier: text('source_identifier').notNull(),
    contentSha256: text('content_sha256').notNull(),
    normalized: jsonb('normalized').$type<unknown>().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('spec_versions_api_hash_uq').on(t.apiId, t.contentSha256),
    check('spec_versions_sha256_format', sql`${t.contentSha256} ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'`),
    index('spec_versions_api_id_idx').on(t.apiId),
  ],
);

/** Every spec check, including failures (FR-11: a failed check is not "no change"). */
export const specChecks = pgTable(
  'spec_checks',
  {
    id: id(),
    apiId: uuid('api_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => apis.id),
    startedAt: tstz('started_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
    finishedAt: tstz('finished_at'),
    outcome: specCheckOutcomeEnum('outcome').notNull(),
    specVersionId: uuid('spec_version_id').references(() => specVersions.id),
    errorDetail: text('error_detail'),
  },
  (t) => [
    check(
      'spec_checks_failed_has_error_detail',
      sql`${t.outcome} <> 'failed' OR ${t.errorDetail} IS NOT NULL`,
    ),
    index('spec_checks_api_id_idx').on(t.apiId),
    index('spec_checks_spec_version_id_idx').on(t.specVersionId),
  ],
);

/** Migration notes for a spec pair (FR-09). */
export const migrationNotes = pgTable(
  'migration_notes',
  {
    id: id(),
    apiId: uuid('api_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => apis.id),
    fromVersionId: uuid('from_version_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => specVersions.id),
    toVersionId: uuid('to_version_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => specVersions.id),
    rawYaml: text('raw_yaml').notNull(),
    parsed: jsonb('parsed').$type<unknown>().notNull(),
    uploadedAt: tstz('uploaded_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('migration_notes_api_id_idx').on(t.apiId),
    index('migration_notes_from_version_id_idx').on(t.fromVersionId),
    index('migration_notes_to_version_id_idx').on(t.toVersionId),
  ],
);

/** One classified spec difference, linked to its evidence (FR-06 to FR-10, DR-02). */
export const changes = pgTable(
  'changes',
  {
    id: id(),
    apiId: uuid('api_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => apis.id),
    fromVersionId: uuid('from_version_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => specVersions.id),
    toVersionId: uuid('to_version_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => specVersions.id),
    category: changeCategoryEnum('category').notNull(),
    oasdiffRuleId: text('oasdiff_rule_id').notNull(),
    classification: classificationEnum('classification').notNull(),
    confidence: doublePrecision('confidence').notNull(),
    path: text('path').notNull(),
    method: text('method'),
    jsonPointer: text('json_pointer').notNull(),
    evidenceBefore: jsonb('evidence_before').$type<unknown>(),
    evidenceAfter: jsonb('evidence_after').$type<unknown>(),
    mappingStatus: mappingStatusEnum('mapping_status').notNull().default('unconfirmed'),
    mappingSource: mappingSourceEnum('mapping_source'),
    mapping: jsonb('mapping').$type<unknown>(),
    migrationNoteId: uuid('migration_note_id').references(() => migrationNotes.id),
    confirmedBy: text('confirmed_by'),
    confirmedAt: tstz('confirmed_at'),
    createdAt: tstz('created_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('changes_confidence_0_1', sql`${t.confidence} >= 0 AND ${t.confidence} <= 1`),
    check(
      'changes_confirmed_iff_source',
      sql`(${t.mappingStatus} = 'confirmed') = (${t.mappingSource} IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      'changes_human_source_has_confirmer',
      sql`${t.mappingSource} IS DISTINCT FROM 'human' OR (${t.confirmedBy} IS NOT NULL AND ${t.confirmedAt} IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
    check('changes_versions_differ', sql`${t.fromVersionId} <> ${t.toVersionId}`),
    index('changes_api_id_idx').on(t.apiId),
    index('changes_from_version_id_idx').on(t.fromVersionId),
    index('changes_to_version_id_idx').on(t.toVersionId),
    index('changes_migration_note_id_idx').on(t.migrationNoteId),
  ],
);

/** One analysis of one change against one repository commit (DR-02). Frozen once finished (FR-39). */
export const runs = pgTable(
  'runs',
  {
    id: id(),
    changeId: uuid('change_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => changes.id),
    repositoryId: uuid('repository_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => repositories.id),
    commitSha: text('commit_sha').notNull(),
    parentRunId: uuid('parent_run_id').references((): AnyPgColumn => runs.id),
    stageReached: stageEnum('stage_reached').notNull(),
    status: runStatusEnum('status').notNull().default('running'),
    failureStage: stageEnum('failure_stage'),
    failureDetail: text('failure_detail'),
    flaggedReason: text('flagged_reason'),
    attemptCount: integer('attempt_count').notNull().default(0),
    tokensIn: integer('tokens_in').notNull().default(0),
    tokensOut: integer('tokens_out').notNull().default(0),
    costUsd: money('cost_usd').notNull().default('0'),
    modelId: text('model_id'),
    isFixture: boolean('is_fixture').notNull().default(false),
    prUrl: text('pr_url'),
    startedAt: tstz('started_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
    finishedAt: tstz('finished_at'),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('runs_attempt_count_0_4', sql`${t.attemptCount} BETWEEN 0 AND 4`),
    check(
      'runs_stage_failure_has_stage',
      sql`${t.status} <> 'stage_failure' OR ${t.failureStage} IS NOT NULL`,
    ),
    check(
      'runs_running_iff_unfinished',
      sql`(${t.status} = 'running') = (${t.finishedAt} IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      'runs_finished_after_start',
      sql`${t.finishedAt} IS NULL OR ${t.finishedAt} >= ${t.startedAt}`,
    ),
    check('runs_commit_sha_format', sql`${t.commitSha} ~ '^[0-9a-f]{40}([0-9a-f]{24})?$'`),
    check(
      'runs_usage_non_negative',
      sql`${t.tokensIn} >= 0 AND ${t.tokensOut} >= 0 AND ${t.costUsd} >= 0`,
    ),
    check('runs_not_own_parent', sql`${t.parentRunId} IS NULL OR ${t.parentRunId} <> ${t.id}`),
    index('runs_change_id_idx').on(t.changeId),
    index('runs_repository_id_idx').on(t.repositoryId),
    index('runs_parent_run_id_idx').on(t.parentRunId),
  ],
);

/** The result each stage wrote before the next began (FR-38). Append-only. */
export const runStageResults = pgTable(
  'run_stage_results',
  {
    id: id(),
    runId: uuid('run_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => runs.id),
    stage: stageEnum('stage').notNull(),
    status: stageResultStatusEnum('status').notNull(),
    result: jsonb('result').$type<unknown>().notNull(),
    startedAt: tstz('started_at').notNull(),
    finishedAt: tstz('finished_at').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('run_stage_results_run_stage_uq').on(t.runId, t.stage),
    check('run_stage_results_finished_after_start', sql`${t.finishedAt} >= ${t.startedAt}`),
  ],
);

/** Located call sites (FR-14). Append-only. */
export const callSites = pgTable(
  'call_sites',
  {
    id: id(),
    runId: uuid('run_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => runs.id),
    filePath: text('file_path').notNull(),
    line: integer('line').notNull(),
    column: integer('column').notNull(),
    pattern: callPatternEnum('pattern').notNull(),
    excerpt: text('excerpt').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('call_sites_position_positive', sql`${t.line} >= 1 AND ${t.column} >= 1`),
    index('call_sites_run_id_idx').on(t.runId),
  ],
);

/** Which files were inspected or skipped, and why (FR-15). Append-only. */
export const coverageEntries = pgTable(
  'coverage_entries',
  {
    id: id(),
    runId: uuid('run_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => runs.id),
    filePath: text('file_path').notNull(),
    inspected: boolean('inspected').notNull(),
    skipReason: text('skip_reason'),
  },
  (t) => [
    check(
      'coverage_entries_skipped_has_reason',
      sql`${t.inspected} OR ${t.skipReason} IS NOT NULL`,
    ),
    index('coverage_entries_run_id_idx').on(t.runId),
  ],
);

/** Repair attempts: at most 4 per run (DR-03); cheating names its guard (DR-04). Append-only. */
export const attempts = pgTable(
  'attempts',
  {
    id: id(),
    runId: uuid('run_id')
      .notNull()
      .references(() => runs.id),
    attemptNumber: integer('attempt_number').notNull(),
    patch: text('patch').notNull(),
    validationOutput: text('validation_output'),
    compileErrors: integer('compile_errors'),
    testsPassed: integer('tests_passed'),
    testsTotal: integer('tests_total'),
    outcome: attemptOutcomeEnum('outcome').notNull(),
    guardName: guardNameEnum('guard_name'),
    tokensIn: integer('tokens_in').notNull(),
    tokensOut: integer('tokens_out').notNull(),
    costUsd: money('cost_usd').notNull(),
    modelId: text('model_id').notNull(),
    durationMs: integer('duration_ms').notNull(),
    createdAt: tstz('created_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('attempts_run_attempt_uq').on(t.runId, t.attemptNumber),
    check('attempts_number_1_4', sql`${t.attemptNumber} BETWEEN 1 AND 4`),
    check(
      'attempts_cheating_iff_guard',
      sql`(${t.outcome} = 'cheating') = (${t.guardName} IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      'attempts_tests_consistent',
      sql`(${t.testsPassed} IS NULL OR ${t.testsPassed} >= 0) AND (${t.testsTotal} IS NULL OR ${t.testsTotal} >= 0) AND (${t.testsPassed} IS NULL OR ${t.testsTotal} IS NULL OR ${t.testsPassed} <= ${t.testsTotal})`,
    ),
    check(
      'attempts_usage_non_negative',
      sql`${t.tokensIn} >= 0 AND ${t.tokensOut} >= 0 AND ${t.costUsd} >= 0 AND ${t.durationMs} >= 0 AND (${t.compileErrors} IS NULL OR ${t.compileErrors} >= 0)`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** Evaluation results per scenario and baseline (SRS 6, EV-07, EV-08). */
export const evaluationResults = pgTable(
  'evaluation_results',
  {
    id: id(),
    evalLabel: text('eval_label').notNull(),
    scenarioId: text('scenario_id').notNull(),
    baseline: baselineEnum('baseline').notNull(),
    outcome: runStatusEnum('outcome').notNull(),
    attemptsUsed: integer('attempts_used').notNull(),
    costUsd: money('cost_usd').notNull(),
    durationMs: integer('duration_ms').notNull(),
    modelMs: integer('model_ms'),
    containerMs: integer('container_ms'),
    reviewVerdict: reviewVerdictEnum('review_verdict').notNull().default('not_reviewed'),
    excluded: boolean('excluded').notNull().default(false),
    exclusionReason: text('exclusion_reason'),
    isPartial: boolean('is_partial').notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: tstz('created_at').notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('evaluation_results_label_scenario_baseline_uq').on(
      t.evalLabel,
      t.scenarioId,
      t.baseline,
    ),
    check(
      'evaluation_results_excluded_has_reason',
      sql`NOT ${t.excluded} OR ${t.exclusionReason} IS NOT NULL`,
    ),
    check('evaluation_results_attempts_0_4', sql`${t.attemptsUsed} BETWEEN 0 AND 4`),
    check(
      'evaluation_results_non_negative',
      sql`${t.costUsd} >= 0 AND ${t.durationMs} >= 0 AND (${t.modelMs} IS NULL OR ${t.modelMs} >= 0) AND (${t.containerMs} IS NULL OR ${t.containerMs} >= 0)`,
    ),
  ],
);

 * Shared domain enums: one source of truth for the database (pgEnums), the pipeline and the UI.
 *
 * Every enum is an `as const` array, a derived union type, and a text-label map. The UI must
 * show these labels, never colour alone (FR-42, UI-02).
 */

/** Change categories in scope (C-01), plus `other` for anything outside them. */
export const CHANGE_CATEGORIES = [
  'field_renamed',
  'field_type_changed',
  'required_param_added',
  'endpoint_removed',
  'other',
] as const;
/** One of {@link CHANGE_CATEGORIES}. */
export type ChangeCategory = (typeof CHANGE_CATEGORIES)[number];
/** Text labels for {@link ChangeCategory}. */
export const CHANGE_CATEGORY_LABELS: Readonly<Record<ChangeCategory, string>> = {
  field_renamed: 'Field renamed',
  field_type_changed: 'Field type changed',
  required_param_added: 'Required parameter added',
  endpoint_removed: 'Endpoint removed',
  other: 'Other change',
};

/** Classification of one spec difference (FR-07). */
export const CLASSIFICATIONS = ['breaking', 'safe', 'unknown'] as const;
/** One of {@link CLASSIFICATIONS}. */
export type Classification = (typeof CLASSIFICATIONS)[number];
/** Text labels for {@link Classification}. */
export const CLASSIFICATION_LABELS: Readonly<Record<Classification, string>> = {
  breaking: 'Breaking',
  safe: 'Safe',
  unknown: 'Unknown',
};

/** Whether an old→new mapping is confirmed (FR-10, BR-04). */
export const MAPPING_STATUSES = ['confirmed', 'unconfirmed'] as const;
/** One of {@link MAPPING_STATUSES}. */
export type MappingStatus = (typeof MAPPING_STATUSES)[number];
/** Text labels for {@link MappingStatus}. */
export const MAPPING_STATUS_LABELS: Readonly<Record<MappingStatus, string>> = {
  confirmed: 'Mapping confirmed',
  unconfirmed: 'Mapping unconfirmed',
};

/** Who confirmed a mapping. A mapping is never confirmed by name resemblance (BR-04). */
export const MAPPING_SOURCES = ['migration_note', 'human'] as const;
/** One of {@link MAPPING_SOURCES}. */
export type MappingSource = (typeof MAPPING_SOURCES)[number];
/** Text labels for {@link MappingSource}. */
export const MAPPING_SOURCE_LABELS: Readonly<Record<MappingSource, string>> = {
  migration_note: 'Confirmed by migration note',
  human: 'Confirmed by a human',
};

-- Append-only protection (FR-39), enforced by the database itself.
-- Hand-written: drizzle-kit cannot express triggers.
--
--   attempts, run_stage_results, call_sites, coverage_entries, spec_versions:
--       no UPDATE, no DELETE, no TRUNCATE.
--   runs:    no DELETE / TRUNCATE; no UPDATE once the OLD row is finished (finished_at IS NOT NULL).
--            Reanalysis = a new run with parent_run_id.
--   changes: no DELETE / TRUNCATE; UPDATE may only touch the human-confirmation columns
--            mapping_status, mapping_source, mapping, confirmed_by, confirmed_at.
--
-- Errors use SQLSTATE 'P0001' (raise_exception) and start with "append-only:".

CREATE FUNCTION sma_block_modification() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: % on table "%" is not allowed (FR-39)', TG_OP, TG_TABLE_NAME
    USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
END;
$$;
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE FUNCTION sma_runs_guard_update() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.finished_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: run % is finished and cannot be updated (FR-39); start a reanalysis run with parent_run_id instead', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE FUNCTION sma_changes_guard_update() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  mutable text[] := ARRAY['mapping_status', 'mapping_source', 'mapping', 'confirmed_by', 'confirmed_at'];
BEGIN
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - mutable) IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) - mutable) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: change % may only update mapping confirmation columns (FR-39)', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER attempts_block_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON attempts
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER attempts_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON attempts
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER run_stage_results_block_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON run_stage_results
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER run_stage_results_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON run_stage_results
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER call_sites_block_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON call_sites
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER call_sites_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON call_sites
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER coverage_entries_block_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON coverage_entries
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER coverage_entries_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON coverage_entries
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER spec_versions_block_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON spec_versions
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER spec_versions_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON spec_versions
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER runs_block_delete BEFORE DELETE ON runs
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER runs_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON runs
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER runs_guard_update BEFORE UPDATE ON runs
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_runs_guard_update();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER changes_block_delete BEFORE DELETE ON changes
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER changes_block_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON changes
  FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_block_modification();
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TRIGGER changes_guard_update BEFORE UPDATE ON changes
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sma_changes_guard_update();
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildApp } from './app.js';

describe('GET /health', () => {
  const app = buildApp();

  afterAll(async () => {
    await app.close();
  });

  it('returns 200 { status: "ok" }', async () => {
    const res = await app.inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/health' });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json()).toEqual({ status: 'ok' });
  });

  it('returns 404 for unknown routes', async () => {
    const res = await app.inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/nope' });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });
});

==> Caveats
==> node@22
node@22 is keg-only, which means it was not symlinked into /opt/homebrew,
because this is an alternate version of another formula.

If you need to have node@22 first in your PATH, run:
  printf '%s\n' export\ PATH\=/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/bin:\$PATH >> ~/.zshrc

For compilers to find node@22 you may need to set:
  export LDFLAGS=-L/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/lib
  export CPPFLAGS=-I/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/include

The following node@22 executables are shadowed by linked node commands in /opt/homebrew/bin:
node
Running these by name will not invoke the version provided by this formula.
Run `brew link node@22` to switch the active version to this keg.
Disable this behaviour by setting `HOMEBREW_NO_PATH_SHADOW_CHECK=1`.
Hide these hints with `HOMEBREW_NO_ENV_HINTS=1` (see `man brew`).

/**
 * @typedef {SpecData} SpecFromData
 */

/**
 * @param {unknown} data
 * @returns {Promise<{ loaded: true; fileName: string | null; spec: SpecFromData} | { loaded: false; errorMessage: string }>}
 */
async function loadSpecFromData(data) {
	if (typeof data === "object") {
		return {
			loaded: true,
			fileName: null,
			spec: /** @type {SpecFromData} */ (data),
		};
	}

	if (typeof data !== "string") {
		return {
			loaded: false,
			errorMessage: `Invalid input type. Expected "string" or "object", but got "${typeof data}"`,
		};
	}

	/** @type {string} */
	let sourceData = data;
	/** @type {string | null} */
	let fileName = null;
	// If string doesn't contains \n assuming is a file path
	if (!sourceData.match(/\n/)) {
		try {
			sourceData = await readFile(data, "utf-8");
			fileName = data;
		} catch (_) {
			return {
				loaded: false,
				errorMessage: `Could not read file "${data}"`,
			};
		}
	}

	try {
		return {
			loaded: true,
			fileName,
			spec: /** @type {SpecFromData} */ (parse(sourceData)),
		};
	} catch (_) {
		return {
			loaded: false,
			errorMessage: "Failed to parse input as YAML/JSON",
		};
	}
}

export class Validator {
	/**
	 * @param {import('./index.js').ValidatorOptions} ajvOptions
	 */
	constructor(ajvOptions = {}) {
		// AJV is a bit too strict in its strict validation of openAPI schemas
		// so switch strict mode and validateFormats off
		if (ajvOptions.strict !== "log") {
			ajvOptions.strict = false;
		}
		this.ajvOptions = ajvOptions;
		/** @type {Partial<Record<string, ReturnType<AjvCore['compile']>>>} */
		this.ajvValidators = {};
		/** @type {Record<string, unknown>} */
		this.externalRefs = {};
	}

	static supportedVersions = openApiVersions;

	/** @type {ValidatorDecl['resolveRefs']} */
	resolveRefs(opts = {}) {
		return /** @type {SpecData} */ (
			replaceRefs(this.specification || opts.specification)
		);
	}

	/** @type {ValidatorDecl['addSpecRef']} */
	async addSpecRef(data, uri) {
		const specResult = await loadSpecFromData(data);
		if (!specResult.loaded) {
			throw new Error(specResult.errorMessage);
		}

		const { spec } = specResult;

		const newUri = uri || spec["$id"];
		if (typeof newUri !== "string") {
			throw new Error("uri parameter or $id attribute must be a string");
		}

		spec["$id"] = newUri;
		this.externalRefs[newUri] = spec;
	}

	/** @type {ValidatorDecl['validate']} */
	async validate(data) {
		const specResult = await loadSpecFromData(data);

		if (!specResult.loaded) {
			return {
				valid: false,
				errors: specResult.errorMessage,
			};
		}
		const { spec: specification } = specResult;
		this.specification = specification;

		if (Object.keys(this.externalRefs).length > 0) {
			specification[inlinedRefs] = this.externalRefs;
		}
		const { version, specificationType, specificationVersion } =
			getOpenApiVersion(specification);
		this.version = version;
		this.specificationVersion = specificationVersion;
		this.specificationType = specificationType;
		if (!version) {
			return {
				valid: false,
				errors:
					"Cannot find supported swagger/openapi version in specification, version must be a string.",
			};
		}
		const validateSchema = this.getAjvValidator(version);
		// check if the specification matches the JSONschema
		const schemaResult = validateSchema(specification);
		// check if the references are valid as those can't be validated bu JSONschema
		if (schemaResult) {
			return checkRefs(specification);
		}

		/** @type {Awaited<ReturnType<ValidatorDecl['validate']>>} */

import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import {
  decideCheckOutcome,
  hashSpec,
  normalizeSpec,
  parseSpec,
  SPEC_CHECK_OUTCOME_LABELS,
  SOURCE_MODE_LABELS,
  type IngestResponse,
  type SourceMode,
  type SpecError,
  type Result,
  ok,
  err,
} from '@sma/core';
import {
  type Db,
  getApiById,
  insertSpecVersion,
  findSpecVersionByHash,
  getSpecVersionById,
  setBaselineVersion,
} from '../infra/db/index.js';
import { apis, specVersions, specChecks } from '../infra/db/schema.js';
import { retrieveSpec, type SpecRetriever } from '../infra/spec-source/retrieve.js';
import { containsCredential, redact } from '../infra/spec-source/security.js';

/** Input modes: fixture files are selected by version, never by arbitrary client paths. */
export type IngestSource =
  | { mode: 'uploaded'; text: string; filename?: string }
  | { mode: 'retrieved' }
  | { mode: 'fixture'; version: 'v1' | 'v2' }
  | { mode: 'failed'; error: SpecError };
/** A missing API is a configuration error, not a spec check. */
export class ApiNotFoundError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('API not found');
  }
}

/** Parse first, then serialize persistence per API using a row lock; failed checks preserve baseline. */
export async function ingest(
  db: Db,
  apiId: string,
  source: IngestSource,
  retrieve: SpecRetriever = retrieveSpec,
): Promise<IngestResponse> {
  const api = await getApiById(db, apiId);
  if (!api) throw new ApiNotFoundError();
  const mode: SourceMode = source.mode === 'failed' ? 'uploaded' : source.mode;
  let identifier = 'upload';
  let fetched: Result<string, SpecError>;
  if (source.mode === 'retrieved') {
    identifier = api.specSourceUrl ?? 'unconfigured source';
    fetched = api.specSourceUrl
      ? await retrieve(api.specSourceUrl)
      : err({ code: 'unreachable', message: 'API has no specification URL' });
  } else if (source.mode === 'fixture') {
    identifier = `demo/api/specs/${source.version}.yaml`;
    try {
      fetched = ok(
        await readFile(
          fileURLToPath(new URL(`../../../../${identifier}`, import.meta.url)),
          'utf8',
        ),
      );
    } catch {
      fetched = err({ code: 'unreachable', message: 'Demo fixture is unavailable' });
    }
  } else if (source.mode === 'failed') {
    fetched = err(source.error);
  } else {
    identifier = source.filename ? `upload:${redact(source.filename).slice(0, 200)}` : 'upload';
    fetched = ok(source.text);
  }
  const parsed = !fetched.ok
    ? fetched
    : containsCredential(fetched.value)
      ? err({
          code: 'invalid_openapi' as const,
          message: 'Remove credential values from specification',
        })
      : await parseSpec(fetched.value);
  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const locked = (await tx.select().from(apis).where(eq(apis.id, apiId)).for('update'))[0];
    if (!locked) throw new ApiNotFoundError();
    let baselineVersionId = locked.baselineVersionId;
    let versionId: string | null = null;
    let outcome: 'baseline_established' | 'no_change' | 'changed' | 'failed' = 'failed';
    let errorDetail: string | null = null;
    if (!parsed.ok)
      errorDetail = redact(`${parsed.error.code}: ${parsed.error.message}`).slice(0, 4000);
    else {
      const canonical = normalizeSpec(parsed.value);
      const hash = hashSpec(canonical);
      const existing = await findSpecVersionByHash(tx, apiId, hash);
      const baseline = baselineVersionId
        ? await getSpecVersionById(tx, baselineVersionId)
        : undefined;
      outcome = decideCheckOutcome({
        baselineHash: baseline?.contentSha256 ?? null,
        newHash: hash,
        existingHashes: existing ? [hash] : [],
      });
      const version =
        existing ??
        (await insertSpecVersion(tx, {
          apiId,
          sourceMode: mode,
          sourceIdentifier: redact(identifier),
          contentSha256: hash,
          normalized: JSON.parse(canonical) as unknown,
        }));
      versionId = version.id;
      if (outcome === 'baseline_established') {
        await setBaselineVersion(tx, apiId, version.id);
        baselineVersionId = version.id;
      }
    }
    const check = (
      await tx
        .insert(specChecks)
        .values({ apiId, outcome, specVersionId: versionId, errorDetail, finishedAt: sql`now()` })
        .returning()
    )[0];
    if (!check) throw new Error('Could not record specification check');
    return {
      check: {
        ...check,
        startedAt: check.startedAt.toISOString(),
        finishedAt: check.finishedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
        outcome: { value: outcome, label: SPEC_CHECK_OUTCOME_LABELS[outcome] },
      },
      baselineVersionId,
      sourceMode: { value: mode, label: SOURCE_MODE_LABELS[mode] },
    };
  });
}

/** Explicit future-orchestrator operation; only a version owned by this API can be selected. */
export async function advanceBaseline(db: Db, apiId: string, versionId: string): Promise<void> {
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const api = (await tx.select().from(apis).where(eq(apis.id, apiId)).for('update'))[0];
    if (!api) throw new ApiNotFoundError();
    const version = (
      await tx
        .select()
        .from(specVersions)
        .where(and(eq(specVersions.id, versionId), eq(specVersions.apiId, apiId)))
    )[0];
    if (!version) throw new Error('Baseline version must belong to this API');
    await setBaselineVersion(tx, apiId, version.id);
  });
}

  "extends": "./tsconfig.json",
  "exclude": ["src/**/*.test.ts"]
}

  "extends": "./tsconfig.json",
  "compilerOptions": {
    "rootDir": "src"

'use strict'

const { performance } = require('node:perf_hooks')

const {
  FST_ERR_LOG_LOGGER_AND_LOGGER_INSTANCE_PROVIDED,
  FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER_CONFIG,
  FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER_INSTANCE,
  FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER,
  FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOG_CONTROLLER
} = require('./errors')
const {
  kLogController
} = require('./symbols.js')
const { LogController } = require('./log-controller.js')

/**
 * Utility for creating a child logger with the appropriate bindings, logger factory
 * and validation.
 * @param {object} context
 * @param {import('../fastify').FastifyBaseLogger} logger
 * @param {import('../fastify').RawRequestDefaultExpression<any>} req
 * @param {string} reqId
 * @param {import('../types/logger.js').ChildLoggerOptions?} loggerOpts
 *
 * @returns {object} New logger instance, inheriting all parent bindings,
 * with child bindings added.
 */
function createChildLogger (context, logger, req, reqId, loggerOpts) {
  const loggerBindings = {
    [context.server[kLogController].requestIdLogLabel]: reqId
  }
  const child = context.childLoggerFactory.call(context.server, logger, loggerBindings, loggerOpts || {}, req)

  // Optimization: bypass validation if the factory is our own default factory
  if (context.childLoggerFactory !== defaultChildLoggerFactory) {
    validateLogger(child, true) // throw if the child is not a valid logger
  }

  return child
}

/** Default factory to create child logger instance
 *
 * @param {import('../fastify.js').FastifyBaseLogger} logger
 * @param {import('../types/logger.js').Bindings} bindings
 * @param {import('../types/logger.js').ChildLoggerOptions} opts
 *
 * @returns {import('../types/logger.js').FastifyBaseLogger}
 */
function defaultChildLoggerFactory (logger, bindings, opts) {
  return logger.child(bindings, opts)
}

/**
 * Determines if a provided logger object meets the requirements
 * of a Fastify compatible logger.
 *
 * @param {object} logger Object to validate.
 * @param {boolean?} strict `true` if the object must be a logger (always throw if any methods missing)
 *
 * @returns {boolean} `true` when the logger meets the requirements.
 *
 * @throws {FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER} When the logger object is
 * missing required methods.
 */
function validateLogger (logger, strict) {
  const methods = ['info', 'error', 'debug', 'fatal', 'warn', 'trace', 'child']
  const missingMethods = logger
    ? methods.filter(method => !logger[method] || typeof logger[method] !== 'function')
    : methods

  if (!missingMethods.length) {
    return true
  } else if ((missingMethods.length === methods.length) && !strict) {
    return false
  } else {
    throw FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER(missingMethods.join(','))
  }
}

function createLogger (options) {
  if (options.logger && options.loggerInstance) {
    throw new FST_ERR_LOG_LOGGER_AND_LOGGER_INSTANCE_PROVIDED()
  }

  if (!options.loggerInstance && !options.logger) {
    const nullLogger = require('abstract-logging')
    const logger = nullLogger
    logger.child = () => logger
    return { logger, hasLogger: false }
  }

  const { createPinoLogger, serializers } = require('./logger-pino.js')

  // check if the logger instance has all required properties
  if (validateLogger(options.loggerInstance)) {
    const logger = createPinoLogger({
      logger: options.loggerInstance,
      serializers: Object.assign({}, serializers, options.loggerInstance.serializers)
    })
    return { logger, hasLogger: true }
  }

  // if a logger instance is passed to logger, throw an exception
  if (validateLogger(options.logger)) {
    throw FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER_CONFIG()
  }

  if (options.loggerInstance) {
    throw FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOGGER_INSTANCE()
  }

  const localLoggerOptions = {}
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(options.logger) === '[object Object]') {
    Reflect.ownKeys(options.logger).forEach(prop => {
      Object.defineProperty(localLoggerOptions, prop, {
        value: options.logger[prop],
        writable: true,
        enumerable: true,
        configurable: true
      })
    })
  }
  localLoggerOptions.level = localLoggerOptions.level || 'info'
  localLoggerOptions.serializers = Object.assign({}, serializers, localLoggerOptions.serializers)
  options.logger = localLoggerOptions
  const logger = createPinoLogger(options.logger)
  return { logger, hasLogger: true }
}

function createLogController (options) {
  const userController = options.logController
  if (!userController) {
    return new LogController(options)
  }

  if (userController instanceof LogController) {
    return userController
  }

  throw new FST_ERR_LOG_INVALID_LOG_CONTROLLER(typeof userController)
}

function now () {
  return performance.now()
}

module.exports = {
  createChildLogger,
  defaultChildLoggerFactory,
  createLogController,
  createLogger,
  LogController,
  validateLogger,
  now
}

/**
 * HTTP application factory for the Self-Maintaining APIs server.
 *
 * @packageDocumentation
 */
import { registerIngestRoutes, type IngestDependencies } from './http/ingest-routes.js';
import Fastify, { type FastifyInstance, type FastifyServerOptions } from 'fastify';

/** Body returned by `GET /health`. */
export interface HealthResponse {
  status: 'ok';
}

/**
 * Builds the Fastify application without opening a port, so tests can call
 * `app.inject()` directly.
 *
 * @param opts - Fastify server options (for example `{ logger: true }`).
 * @returns A configured, not-yet-listening Fastify instance.
 */
export function buildApp(
  opts: FastifyServerOptions = {},
  deps?: IngestDependencies,
): FastifyInstance {
  const app = Fastify({ ...opts, disableRequestLogging: true });

  app.get('/health', async (): Promise<HealthResponse> => ({ status: 'ok' }));

  if (deps) void app.register(registerIngestRoutes, deps);
  return app;
}

Decisions are kept in **one file per increment** so parallel PRs do not conflict.
Each file is a table with columns: Date | Increment | Decision | Why.

| Increment                            | File                                            |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------- |

| 03b Team workflow                    | [docs/decisions/inc03b.md](decisions/inc03b.md) |

New increments add `docs/decisions/incNN.md` in their own PR and add one row here.
